This design makes state management implicit, creating two common failure modes. An agent may retrieve the right facts but later ground its decision in stale, missing, or incorrect information; and a syntactically valid tool call may still violate a domain policy that depends on the current task state. We introduce \textsc{LedgerAgent}, an inference-time method for tool-calling agents that maintains observed task states in a separate ledger and renders the states into the prompt. The ledger is also used to check state-dependent policy constraints before environment-changing tool calls are executed, blocking policy violations. Across four customer-service domains and a mixed panel of open- and closed-weight models, \textsc{LedgerAgent} improves average pass\textasciicircum{}k over a standard prompt-based tool-calling approach, with the largest gains under stricter multi-trial consistency metrics.

</details>


### [228] [DeepSWIP: Quotient-WMC Counterfactuals for Neural Probabilistic Logic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20526)
*Saimun Habib,Vaishak Belle,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeepSWIP enables exact counterfactual reasoning in DeepProbLog by reducing neural predicates to ProbLog choices and applying SWIPs with WMC, achieving theoretical exactness and empirical speedup.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard inference in neurosymbolic systems like DeepProbLog is associational; counterfactual reasoning requires a causal semantics for interventions and evidence.

Method: DeepSWIP uses neural materialization to reduce fixed-context neural predicates to ordinary ProbLog choices, applies Single World Intervention Programs (SWIPs), and computes counterfactuals via weighted model counting over a single transformed program.

Result: Experiments on MPI3D confirm correctness against DeepTwin with 12,000 queries and a 2.14× inference speedup. A SUMO HOV experiment shows that AIPW estimator removes most first-order bias from neural calibration degradation.

Conclusion: DeepSWIP provides exact and efficient counterfactual reasoning for neurosymbolic programs, with theoretical guarantees and practical benefits in speed and bias correction.

Abstract: Neurosymbolic systems such as DeepProbLog combine neural perception with probabilistic logic, but standard inference is associational. Counterfactual reasoning additionally requires a causal semantics for interventions and evidence. We introduce DeepSWIP, a single-world counterfactual semantics for DeepProbLog programs. Using neural materialization, we reduce fixed-context neural predicates to ordinary ProbLog choices, apply Single World Intervention Programs (SWIPs), and compute counterfactuals by weighted model counting (WMC) over a single transformed program. Under finite grounding and unique-supported-model assumptions, DeepSWIP is exact relative to the learned materialized FCM. The standard quotient-WMC form of ProbLog conditionals identifies active neural probabilities and explains intervention cleaning, calibration sensitivity, and rare-evidence instability. Experiments on MPI3D confirm the transformation against a DeepTwin construction against 12,000 queries, as predicted and a 2.14$\times$ inference speedup from avoiding the Twin's endogenous duplication. A SUMO HOV experiment shows that neural calibration degradation biases plug-in estimates, while a correctly scoped randomized-policy AIPW estimator removes most first-order bias for population mean and ATE estimands. Code is at .

</details>


### [229] [How Do Instructions Shape Speech? Cross-Attention Attribution for Style-Captioned Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20532)
*Nityanand Mathur,Hamees Sayed,Wasim Madha,Apoorv Singh,Sameer Khurana,Akshat Mandloi,Sudarshan Kamath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首次将DAAM框架应用于语音扩散模型，分析风格描述对语音生成的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解单词如何影响声学输出，以诊断故障并提高可控性。

Method: 提出了面向语音扩散模型的交叉注意力归因方法，首次将DAAM框架应用于语音领域，并在CapSpeech-TTS上实现了每个token的热力图提取，涵盖25层和24个ODE步骤。

Result: 风格token的时间方差低于内容/功能token；风格注意力与F0和能量相关；风格调节在早期步骤和深层达到峰值；注意力熵在第17层最小，与风格重要性峰值同时出现，表明在风格关键阶段网络选择性最大。

Conclusion: 这是首次研究自然语言如何影响语音扩散模型中的交叉注意力。

Abstract: Style-captioned text-to-speech systems use natural language to control voice characteristics, but how individual words influence acoustic output remains unclear. Understanding this is critical for diagnosing failure modes and improving controllability in expressive TTS. We propose cross-attention attribution for speech diffusion models, adapting the DAAM framework to the speech domain for the first time, and apply it to CapSpeech-TTS. Our method extracts per-token heatmaps across 25 layers and 24 ODE steps. We analyze 3,600 (style caption, text transcript) combinations comprising 120 style captions conditioning the generation of 30 text transcripts each, revealing how caption tokens shape waveforms. Results show: (1) style tokens have lower temporal variance than content/function tokens, confirming global conditioning; (2) style attention correlates with F0 and energy; (3) style conditioning peaks in early steps and deep layers; (4) attention entropy reaches its minimum at layer 17, co-occurring with the style importance peak, indicating maximal network selectivity at the most style-critical stage. This is the first study of how natural language influences cross-attention in speech diffusion models

</details>


### [230] [Toward Calibrated Mixture-of-Experts Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20544)
*Gina Wong,Drew Prinster,Suchi Saria,Rama Chellappa,Anqi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究混合专家模型在分布偏移下的校准问题，提出对抗性重新加权方法改善校准与准确率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校准帮助混合专家模型的条件尚不明确，需要研究路由机制与专家级校准的交互。

Method: 提出对抗性重新加权方法，惩罚分布偏移下路由聚合的校准误差。

Result: 专家校准足以确保硬路由模型在分布偏移下的整体校准，但对软路由不足；新方法改善了准确率-校准权衡。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效改进混合专家模型在分布偏移下的校准，并提升准确率-校准权衡。

Abstract: Calibration aligns a model's predictive uncertainty with the frequencies of its empirical outcomes and is important for understanding and trusting reported probabilities. Recent work shows that enforcing calibration at the level of individual predictors can improve ensemble accuracy and calibration, with mixture-of-experts (MoE) models showing strong empirical improvements in particular; however, the conditions under which calibration helps MoE are not well understood. In this work, we study how MoE models behave under distribution shift, focusing on how routing mechanisms interact with expert-level calibration. We show that expert calibration is sufficient to ensure calibration of the overall model under a broad class of distribution shifts in hard-routed models, but is insufficient for calibrating soft-routed models. To address this, we propose an adversarial reweighting that penalizes calibration errors of the routed aggregate under distribution shift, and we demonstrate that it improves the accuracy-calibration tradeoff both on average and on difficult subsets of the data, across model classes, prediction tasks, and distribution shifts.

</details>
